./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g3-cas-store.txt:90:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:34:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:144:| Decision | Worker execution claim 与 repository writer lease 是不同机制。Async writer lease 以 `(RepositoryId, StoreKind)` 为 scope，持久化 owner operation、单调递增 fencing token、acquired/renewed/expiry timestamps；effect 前及 commit/ref/journal evidence publication 前均校验 token。Lease expiry、heartbeat timeout、worker loss 或 HTTP disconnect 只触发 RECONCILING，不自动授权下一 writer。Takeover 必须先确保旧 Dolt runtime 已停止、drain 或被目录 ownership 隔离，再检查 journal 与实际 store evidence：只有明确 NOT_APPLIED 才可继续或重放，APPLIED 转 verification，UNKNOWN 保持占用并调查。Dolt 与 Current 不同 StoreKind 可并发，同 store writer 串行。Current 小型同步 mutation 仅使用短 SQLite transaction、generation CAS 和数据库 writer lock；异步 Current replace/bulk 才获取 Current store lease。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g3-cas-store.txt:93:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:37:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:221:| Decision | Repository Service是T2 journal/store evidence reconciler的唯一owner；Business Operation Runtime只能查询Repository operation/evidence，不直接读取Dolt目录推断outcome。Accepted worker、lease takeover、service restart、bounded periodic scan和operator recheck均可触发同一reconciler。Startup按global registry进行有界、分片、增量的per-repository control scan，不一次加载全部Dolt repository；同一operation只有一个reconciliation claim并服从store lease/fencing。UNKNOWN默认阻塞相同`(RepositoryId, StoreKind)`的后续mutation，不阻塞其他repository或同repository另一StoreKind；storage健康时，独立有效的immutable pinned reads可继续。READBACK_INTEGRITY_MISMATCH或store corruption使受影响StoreKind unavailable，另一StoreKind保持可用。单repository故障不使全服务unready。自动reconciliation耗尽有界策略后进入ATTENTION_REQUIRED，但保留原effect与occupancy。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g3-cas-store.txt:94:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:38:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:281:| Decision | Per-repository control SQLite缺失、损坏、schema不兼容或无法通过integrity validation时，受影响repository fail closed为unavailable；不得仅从Dolt/current data自动重建operation authority，只能通过完整repository aggregate restore或受控operator evidence-preserving repair恢复。Journal与store evidence矛盾时不设置任一方自动获胜规则：journal result commit与internal ref不同、Current effect marker digest与journal不同、VERIFIED但必要commit/readback evidence缺失等均为`INTEGRITY_FAILURE/OPERATOR_INTERVENTION`，保留冲突双方evidence并quarantine受影响StoreKind，停止其普通读写与cleanup；另一StoreKind和其他repository继续运行。绕过Repository Service直接修改Dolt、SQLite或durable root均属unsupported out-of-band mutation，发现后按integrity failure处理。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g3-cas-store.txt:245:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:401:| Decision | 必须区分“requested content/structure verification未匹配”与“Repository authority自相矛盾”。SubmitImport或async Current content write的STRUCTURAL/FULL检查不匹配时，mutation effect仍记录为APPLIED，operation以typed verification failure结束而不进入VERIFIED/SUCCEEDED；它可以呈现`FAILED + effect=APPLIED`，writer lease和Repository mutation lane在failure evidence持久化后释放，禁止自动重放，caller自行决定后续write或业务处理。该失败本身不使StoreKind unavailable、不创建integrity overlay，也不追溯修改其他operation。独立只读VerifyCommit mismatch仅使该verification operation FAILED、effect=NONE，原import/业务结果不变。只有journal、internal ref、commit marker、Current effect marker或provider storage evidence彼此矛盾、缺失authority identity或证明实际storage corruption时，才使用INTEGRITY_FAILURE、quarantine和operator intervention。Known verification failure终态后可按Round 7 retention清理transient staging/internal ref，但必须保留journal、result commit/target evidence、request digest和failure evidence。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g3-cas-store.txt:249:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:356:| Decision | Required matrix按caller-selected policy而非旧strict-only规则验收。Versioned与async Current均覆盖`NONE/STRUCTURAL/FULL`成功；STRUCTURAL/FULL分别覆盖其应检测的mismatch；NONE须证明不执行内容readback但仍保存journal/effect attribution/recovery evidence；独立`VerifyCommit`覆盖成功与普通失败且不得改写原operation。Mutable Current、SubmitImport target publication和mutable ref update/delete必须显式覆盖`CHECK_EXPECTED`成功/stale conflict及`LAST_WRITE_WINS`覆盖结果；缺失`concurrency_mode`是schema rejection。Versioned import在两种mode下仍须base CommitId；immutable/protected/internal/operation ref及name uniqueness拒绝LWW。两种mode均验证idempotency、journal、lease/fencing和crash recovery。Schema-valid typed rejection、verification failure、UNKNOWN/ATTENTION等若与golden匹配，scenario为PASSED；无法形成可信observation、fault/barrier失败或cleanup泄漏为HARNESS_ERROR。Runner禁止automatic retry；显式同key retry是scenario stimulus并保留首次evidence。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:40:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:314:| Constraints | Caller choice只能降低内容/结构verification，不能关闭authentication/authorization、input safety、idempotency、atomic effect publication、writer fencing、effect evidence或reconciliation。所有consumer不得把裸VERIFIED解释为FULL，必须读取completed level；需要FULL的业务caller自行请求并检查。CurrentTableStore的transactional mutation correctness不受本decision降级。该decision显式替代`P2-R4-D01`、`P2-R5-D01`、`P2-R6-D02`、`P2-R6-D05`、`P2-R6-D06`和`P2-R6-D07`中“normal import必须full readback才可VERIFIED”及“VERIFIED必然存在full SnapshotManifest”的局部条款，并扩展`P2-R2-D03`的closed action surface加入VerifyCommit；其余语义保持ACCEPTED。对应Accepted formal contracts与ADR必须在整体formalization时显式supersede，不得静默兼容两套VERIFIED语义。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:41:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:341:| Decision | Mutable write必须显式、无default地选择`concurrency_mode = CHECK_EXPECTED | LAST_WRITE_WINS`。CHECK_EXPECTED要求observed generation/commit并在同一effect boundary校验；LAST_WRITE_WINS不要求expected token，caller承担覆盖并发更新的风险。该选择适用于Current Replace/Apply/Drop、SubmitImport的mutable target publication和mutable ref update/delete；Versioned import仍必须提供base CommitId定义commit parent/content，LAST_WRITE_WINS只放宽target publication。Ref surface保留CompareAndSetRef并新增显式SetRef或等价closed LWW action，不把缺失expected token隐式解释为LWW。Create名称唯一性、immutable ref、protected/internal ref、operation ref及technical metadata不允许LWW绕过。所有模式仍使用idempotency、journal和effect evidence。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:45:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:401:| Decision | 必须区分“requested content/structure verification未匹配”与“Repository authority自相矛盾”。SubmitImport或async Current content write的STRUCTURAL/FULL检查不匹配时，mutation effect仍记录为APPLIED，operation以typed verification failure结束而不进入VERIFIED/SUCCEEDED；它可以呈现`FAILED + effect=APPLIED`，writer lease和Repository mutation lane在failure evidence持久化后释放，禁止自动重放，caller自行决定后续write或业务处理。该失败本身不使StoreKind unavailable、不创建integrity overlay，也不追溯修改其他operation。独立只读VerifyCommit mismatch仅使该verification operation FAILED、effect=NONE，原import/业务结果不变。只有journal、internal ref、commit marker、Current effect marker或provider storage evidence彼此矛盾、缺失authority identity或证明实际storage corruption时，才使用INTEGRITY_FAILURE、quarantine和operator intervention。Known verification failure终态后可按Round 7 retention清理transient staging/internal ref，但必须保留journal、result commit/target evidence、request digest和failure evidence。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:118:### P2-R0-D06 — Additive CurrentTableStore scope
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:125:| Decision | Phase 2 仍以独立、通用、容器化的 Repository Service foundation 为边界，并新增 optional generic `CurrentTableStore` capability。VersionedTableStore/Dolt 与 CurrentTableStore 独立处理 caller-owned tabular content；Repository Service 不理解 Project、Workspace、Submission 或表的业务语义，不决定哪一侧是业务 authority，也不保证跨 store 内容一致。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:126:| Rationale | Caller 的实际数据模型同时需要版本化表与 latest-only 表；把 CurrentTableStore 作为显式隔离的附加能力，可复用 RepositoryId、部署、安全和 artifact 基础设施，而不把服务扩张为业务状态机或双写协调器。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:141:| Decision | 在保留原 Phase 2 non-goals 的基础上，明确 CurrentTableStore 不提供业务 persistence-policy authority、自动 store routing、跨 store transaction/consistency/synchronization、跨 Dolt/SQLite join、通用 query federation、任意 SQL/DDL、业务 workflow/state machine 或 v1 convenience dual-write。服务可报告两侧独立 inventory，但不得推断 authority、同步或等价关系。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:307:| Topic | Deployment/runtime ownership after CurrentTableStore amendment |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:309:| Decision | 每个 backend environment 仍使用一个逻辑 Repository Service endpoint；一个 disposable active container/process 独占一个 trust-domain durable root，并管理多个 RepositoryId。每个 RepositoryId 的 durable aggregate 包含独立的 VersionedTableStore/Dolt、按需创建的 CurrentTableStore/current database、technical control store 和 staging/artifacts。Current table data 与 technical registry 必须分离。Repository lifecycle、backup inventory、quota 和 deletion 识别全部组件，但不声明 Dolt/current 是一致快照。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:311:| Rejected alternatives | 所有 repository 共用一个 current SQLite 数据库：形成共享 writer/failure domain；CurrentTableStore 独立服务：首版增加第二套路由、认证、部署和恢复；每项目动态容器：需要未设计的 sharding control plane。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:315:| Reopen condition | Accepted capacity/availability requirement 需要 repository sharding 或独立 CurrentTableStore service，且 routing/fencing/control-plane 已单独设计。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:323:| Topic | Writer serialization after CurrentTableStore amendment |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:325:| Decision | Semantic writer serialization scope 为 `(RepositoryId, StoreKind)`。同 repository 的 VersionedTableStore writes 串行；同 repository 的 CurrentTableStore writes 串行；两种 store 的 writes 可彼此并发，因为不存在 cross-store transaction/consistency。Dolt read 绑定 immutable CommitId，current read 绑定稳定 CurrentGenerationId，均可与各自后续 write 并发。不同 repository operations 在 bounded node budget 内并发。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:341:| Decision | Repository Service 两种 store 共享 table-oriented、bulk-capable、bounded resource、single writer per `(RepositoryId, StoreKind)`、streaming read、typed mutation、无任意 SQL/跨表或跨 store transaction 的 workload envelope；store profile 保持不同：VersionedTableStore 面向 async bulk import/commit/ref/CAS 和 immutable history read，CurrentTableStore 面向 async bulk replace 加 bounded row/field mutation 和 latest-generation read。两侧不承诺相同 mutation capability或写延迟。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:343:| Rejected alternatives | 两侧完全相同 CRUD/concurrency/SLA：忽略引擎语义；把 CurrentTableStore 扩成高并发 OLTP：超出 SQLite/single-writer和本服务边界；完全分离资源治理：无法保护共享节点。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:461:| Decision | Public API 将 VersionedTableStore 与 CurrentTableStore 暴露为显式、独立的 typed operation families；caller 在每次调用中选择 store，并在自己的 owner contract 保存 table persistence policy。Repository Service 不保存或解释该 policy，不提供按 table name 自动选路的统一读写接口。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:465:| Downstream impact | 新增 CurrentTableStore port/wire schema/SDK actions；application adapter 负责 persistence policy 与显式 route；authorization、metrics 和 fixtures 必须按 store/action 区分。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:476:| Decision | 同一 RepositoryId 下允许同名表分别存在于 VersionedTableStore 与 CurrentTableStore，内容可不同。Inventory/catalog 按 store 分别报告 `table_name`、`store_kind`、`exists`、schema identity，以及适用的 CommitId 或 CurrentGenerationId；两侧存在时返回独立 entry，不合并为一张逻辑表，不报告 authority、synchronized 或 equivalent。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:484:### P2-R2-D08 — Typed CurrentTableStore API
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:491:| Decision | v1 CurrentTableStore 提供显式 typed operations：CreateCurrentTable、GetCurrentCatalog、ReplaceCurrentTable、UpsertCurrentTableRows、DeleteCurrentTableRows、StreamCurrentTableRows、DropCurrentTable。Schema、key、projection、predicate 和 row payload 使用 versioned DTO/artifact/stream contract；禁止 arbitrary SQL、arbitrary DDL、PRAGMA、extension loading 和直接数据库连接/文件访问。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:507:| Decision | v1 不提供同时写 VersionedTableStore 与 CurrentTableStore 的 convenience/composite API。Caller 发起两个独立请求并拥有 orchestration、一致性判断和恢复策略；服务不生成 aggregate success，也不隐藏 partial outcome。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-0-to-3.md:522:| Decision | CurrentTableStore 以 RepositoryId 为 logical isolation/writer scope，并使用 opaque `CurrentGenerationId` 表示可读取的当前状态 revision；它只支持 equality/CAS binding，不提供历史读取或版本语义。Mutation 可携带 `expected_generation` 防止 lost update；read cursor/stream 必须绑定稳定 generation。首版 storage 采用每 RepositoryId 独立 current SQLite database 的方向，禁止所有 repository 共用一个 SQLite writer domain。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g1-verification-level.txt:22:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:314:| Constraints | Caller choice只能降低内容/结构verification，不能关闭authentication/authorization、input safety、idempotency、atomic effect publication、writer fencing、effect evidence或reconciliation。所有consumer不得把裸VERIFIED解释为FULL，必须读取completed level；需要FULL的业务caller自行请求并检查。CurrentTableStore的transactional mutation correctness不受本decision降级。该decision显式替代`P2-R4-D01`、`P2-R5-D01`、`P2-R6-D02`、`P2-R6-D05`、`P2-R6-D06`和`P2-R6-D07`中“normal import必须full readback才可VERIFIED”及“VERIFIED必然存在full SnapshotManifest”的局部条款，并扩展`P2-R2-D03`的closed action surface加入VerifyCommit；其余语义保持ACCEPTED。对应Accepted formal contracts与ADR必须在整体formalization时显式supersede，不得静默兼容两套VERIFIED语义。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:189:| Decision | `/livez`只证明process/event loop能够及时响应，不探测Dolt、全部repository、backup target或普通dependency；`/readyz`表示服务可安全接纳至少一部分public workload。Global registry不可读、production required identity缺失/不匹配、unknown storage major、control emergency reserve不可用或production auth配置无效时整体not-ready。单repository、单StoreKind、单个Dolt runtime、GC/backup target或per-repository recovery故障只标记对应repository/capability degraded/unavailable，不使全服务not-ready。Startup验证global authority并启动recovery scheduler后即可ready，不等待遍历上万个repository；首次访问尚未检查的repository时，mutation admission前必须完成该repository按需reconciliation。Unauthenticated probes只返回sanitized状态；authenticated operator health返回component、水位、reconciliation backlog、runtime pool、audit collector和capability degradation详情。Legacy `/healthz`如保留，只作为`/readyz`兼容alias，不定义第四套health语义。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:356:| Decision | Required matrix按caller-selected policy而非旧strict-only规则验收。Versioned与async Current均覆盖`NONE/STRUCTURAL/FULL`成功；STRUCTURAL/FULL分别覆盖其应检测的mismatch；NONE须证明不执行内容readback但仍保存journal/effect attribution/recovery evidence；独立`VerifyCommit`覆盖成功与普通失败且不得改写原operation。Mutable Current、SubmitImport target publication和mutable ref update/delete必须显式覆盖`CHECK_EXPECTED`成功/stale conflict及`LAST_WRITE_WINS`覆盖结果；缺失`concurrency_mode`是schema rejection。Versioned import在两种mode下仍须base CommitId；immutable/protected/internal/operation ref及name uniqueness拒绝LWW。两种mode均验证idempotency、journal、lease/fencing和crash recovery。Schema-valid typed rejection、verification failure、UNKNOWN/ATTENTION等若与golden匹配，scenario为PASSED；无法形成可信observation、fault/barrier失败或cleanup泄漏为HARNESS_ERROR。Runner禁止automatic retry；显式同key retry是scenario stimulus并保留首次evidence。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:508:| Decision | Phase 2 implementation使用一个非代码前置`F0 Formal closure`和十个纵向WP：`WP0 delivery skeleton and gates`；可有限并行的`WP1 public contracts/client/service shell`与`WP2 Dolt/SQLite/filesystem feasibility`；随后`WP3 repository durable foundation`、`WP4 operation/journal/recovery runtime`；可有限并行的`WP5 CurrentTableStore`与`WP6 versioned read/runtime`；再串行完成`WP7 versioned write/ref/verification`、`WP8 retention/GC/backup/restore/delete`及`WP9 production hardening/deployment/Phase exit`。F0要求整体设计确认、Consolidated Design Review、用户formalization授权、已知formal冲突显式supersede及blocking closure；F0通过前不得开始实现WP0。每个WP必须交付可执行的纵向能力或交付控制，不建设无consumer的通用framework。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:67:| Decision | 技术状态采用分层SQLite authority。Global registry只保存RepositoryId、lifecycle、layout/format version、内部storage key及create/delete lifecycle/idempotency。每个repository的独立control SQLite保存versioned async operation journal、async idempotency result、lease/fencing和recovery evidence。CurrentTableStore的catalog/table generation及同步mutation idempotency result保存在该repository的current SQLite中，与current data mutation在同一transaction提交。Dolt commit/ref是versioned effect evidence，但不能替代operation journal和VERIFIED readback evidence。JSON/sidecar marker只可作为filesystem reconciliation evidence，不是第二metadata authority。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:72:| Formal owner | Repository registry/storage contract；Repository operation/effect contract；CurrentTableStore transaction contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:144:| Decision | Worker execution claim 与 repository writer lease 是不同机制。Async writer lease 以 `(RepositoryId, StoreKind)` 为 scope，持久化 owner operation、单调递增 fencing token、acquired/renewed/expiry timestamps；effect 前及 commit/ref/journal evidence publication 前均校验 token。Lease expiry、heartbeat timeout、worker loss 或 HTTP disconnect 只触发 RECONCILING，不自动授权下一 writer。Takeover 必须先确保旧 Dolt runtime 已停止、drain 或被目录 ownership 隔离，再检查 journal 与实际 store evidence：只有明确 NOT_APPLIED 才可继续或重放，APPLIED 转 verification，UNKNOWN 保持占用并调查。Dolt 与 Current 不同 StoreKind 可并发，同 store writer 串行。Current 小型同步 mutation 仅使用短 SQLite transaction、generation CAS 和数据库 writer lock；异步 Current replace/bulk 才获取 Current store lease。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:172:| Topic | CurrentTableStore mutation effect, verification and crash recovery |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:179:| Formal owner | CurrentTableStore mutation/transaction contract；Repository operation/effect contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:221:| Decision | Repository Service是T2 journal/store evidence reconciler的唯一owner；Business Operation Runtime只能查询Repository operation/evidence，不直接读取Dolt目录推断outcome。Accepted worker、lease takeover、service restart、bounded periodic scan和operator recheck均可触发同一reconciler。Startup按global registry进行有界、分片、增量的per-repository control scan，不一次加载全部Dolt repository；同一operation只有一个reconciliation claim并服从store lease/fencing。UNKNOWN默认阻塞相同`(RepositoryId, StoreKind)`的后续mutation，不阻塞其他repository或同repository另一StoreKind；storage健康时，独立有效的immutable pinned reads可继续。READBACK_INTEGRITY_MISMATCH或store corruption使受影响StoreKind unavailable，另一StoreKind保持可用。单repository故障不使全服务unready。自动reconciliation耗尽有界策略后进入ATTENTION_REQUIRED，但保留原effect与occupancy。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:281:| Decision | Per-repository control SQLite缺失、损坏、schema不兼容或无法通过integrity validation时，受影响repository fail closed为unavailable；不得仅从Dolt/current data自动重建operation authority，只能通过完整repository aggregate restore或受控operator evidence-preserving repair恢复。Journal与store evidence矛盾时不设置任一方自动获胜规则：journal result commit与internal ref不同、Current effect marker digest与journal不同、VERIFIED但必要commit/readback evidence缺失等均为`INTEGRITY_FAILURE/OPERATOR_INTERVENTION`，保留冲突双方evidence并quarantine受影响StoreKind，停止其普通读写与cleanup；另一StoreKind和其他repository继续运行。绕过Repository Service直接修改Dolt、SQLite或durable root均属unsupported out-of-band mutation，发现后按integrity failure处理。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:301:| Formal owner | Repository lifecycle/registry contract；CurrentTableStore materialization contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:314:| Constraints | Caller choice只能降低内容/结构verification，不能关闭authentication/authorization、input safety、idempotency、atomic effect publication、writer fencing、effect evidence或reconciliation。所有consumer不得把裸VERIFIED解释为FULL，必须读取completed level；需要FULL的业务caller自行请求并检查。CurrentTableStore的transactional mutation correctness不受本decision降级。该decision显式替代`P2-R4-D01`、`P2-R5-D01`、`P2-R6-D02`、`P2-R6-D05`、`P2-R6-D06`和`P2-R6-D07`中“normal import必须full readback才可VERIFIED”及“VERIFIED必然存在full SnapshotManifest”的局部条款，并扩展`P2-R2-D03`的closed action surface加入VerifyCommit；其余语义保持ACCEPTED。对应Accepted formal contracts与ADR必须在整体formalization时显式supersede，不得静默兼容两套VERIFIED语义。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:341:| Decision | Mutable write必须显式、无default地选择`concurrency_mode = CHECK_EXPECTED | LAST_WRITE_WINS`。CHECK_EXPECTED要求observed generation/commit并在同一effect boundary校验；LAST_WRITE_WINS不要求expected token，caller承担覆盖并发更新的风险。该选择适用于Current Replace/Apply/Drop、SubmitImport的mutable target publication和mutable ref update/delete；Versioned import仍必须提供base CommitId定义commit parent/content，LAST_WRITE_WINS只放宽target publication。Ref surface保留CompareAndSetRef并新增显式SetRef或等价closed LWW action，不把缺失expected token隐式解释为LWW。Create名称唯一性、immutable ref、protected/internal ref、operation ref及technical metadata不允许LWW绕过。所有模式仍使用idempotency、journal和effect evidence。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:343:| Rejected alternatives | 所有mutable write强制CHECK_EXPECTED：过度固定caller风险偏好；缺失expected即默认LWW：隐式降级；允许immutable/protected ref force update：破坏资源语义与内部恢复。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:361:| Formal owner | CurrentTableStore mutation/verification contract；Repository operation result contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:391:| Formal owner | CurrentTableStore TableMutationBatch contract。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:401:| Decision | 必须区分“requested content/structure verification未匹配”与“Repository authority自相矛盾”。SubmitImport或async Current content write的STRUCTURAL/FULL检查不匹配时，mutation effect仍记录为APPLIED，operation以typed verification failure结束而不进入VERIFIED/SUCCEEDED；它可以呈现`FAILED + effect=APPLIED`，writer lease和Repository mutation lane在failure evidence持久化后释放，禁止自动重放，caller自行决定后续write或业务处理。该失败本身不使StoreKind unavailable、不创建integrity overlay，也不追溯修改其他operation。独立只读VerifyCommit mismatch仅使该verification operation FAILED、effect=NONE，原import/业务结果不变。只有journal、internal ref、commit marker、Current effect marker或provider storage evidence彼此矛盾、缺失authority identity或证明实际storage corruption时，才使用INTEGRITY_FAILURE、quarantine和operator intervention。Known verification failure终态后可按Round 7 retention清理transient staging/internal ref，但必须保留journal、result commit/target evidence、request digest和failure evidence。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:418:| Decision | Versioned commit 在可从protected/mutable/immutable public ref到达、被nonterminal operation/internal operation ref/recovery evidence引用、被active read/export/backup pin，或仍处于terminal operation的30天diagnostic hold时保留。全部root和hold解除后commit可由GC删除，后续exact lookup返回typed `COMMIT_GONE`。v1不新增通用`RetentionLease`公共资源；caller需要长期保留不能由其他ref到达的commit时创建immutable ref。CurrentTableStore仍只保存当前状态，mutation后旧CurrentTableGenerationId不可用于读取历史内容，也不为GC复制旧SQLite generation。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s9-step-4-old-reference-raw/g1-verification-level.txt:301:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s9-step-4-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:700:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw-rerun/g1-verification-level.txt:5:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g3-cas-store.txt:40:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:314:| Constraints | Caller choice只能降低内容/结构verification，不能关闭authentication/authorization、input safety、idempotency、atomic effect publication、writer fencing、effect evidence或reconciliation。所有consumer不得把裸VERIFIED解释为FULL，必须读取completed level；需要FULL的业务caller自行请求并检查。CurrentTableStore的transactional mutation correctness不受本decision降级。该decision显式替代`P2-R4-D01`、`P2-R5-D01`、`P2-R6-D02`、`P2-R6-D05`、`P2-R6-D06`和`P2-R6-D07`中“normal import必须full readback才可VERIFIED”及“VERIFIED必然存在full SnapshotManifest”的局部条款，并扩展`P2-R2-D03`的closed action surface加入VerifyCommit；其余语义保持ACCEPTED。对应Accepted formal contracts与ADR必须在整体formalization时显式supersede，不得静默兼容两套VERIFIED语义。 |
